.Part III— Sec. 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, APRIL 16, 1994 (CHAITRA 26, 1916) 


2415 


THE INSTITUTE OF CHARTERED 
ACCOUNTANTS OF INDIA 

New Delhi-110002, the 17th March, 1994 

No. 3NCA(4)/3/93-94:—In pursuance of Regu¬ 
lation 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 
1988, it is hereby notified that in exercise of the 
powers conferred by Clause (C) of Sub-Section (1) 
of Section 20 of the Chartered Accountants Act, 
1949, the Council of the Institute of Chartered Ac¬ 
countants of India has removed from the Register 
of Members of this Institute on account of non¬ 
payment of the prescribed fees, the names of the 
following members with effect from 1st October, 
1993.

Act) :

or payment , of premium, in enjoyment of benefits under the

favourable to such employees that the benefits admissible 
under the Employees’ Deposit Linked Insurance Scheme, 
1976 fhereinafter referred to as the said scheme) :

SCHEDULE-I 

t - < 

1. Th^ employer in Ration to each of the said establish¬ 
ment (hereinafter ; referred to as the employer) shall submit 
such repif-ns to tb? .Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
concerned and maintain such accounts and provide such faci¬ 
lities for inspection, os the Central Provident Fund Commis¬ 
sioner may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such Inspection charges as the 
Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
clause (a) of sub-Section (2A) of Section 17 of the said Act, 
wtihin 15 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the Group 
Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, sub¬ 
mission of returns, payment of insuronce premia, transfer 
of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc, shall be borne 
by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insurance 
Scheme as approved by the Central Government/Central 
Provident Fund Commissioner as and when amended, along- 
\yith translation of the salient features thereof in the language 
of majority of the employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already a member of the 
Employee’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in 
his establishment the employer shall immediately admit him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary Premium in respect of him to the Life Insuronce Corpo¬ 
ration of India. 

13 —29G1/94 


2443 

6. The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance 
Scheme appropriately if the benefits available to the emp¬ 
loyees under the said Scheme arc enhanced so that the bene¬ 
fits available Linder the Group Insurance Scheme are more 
favourable to the employees than the benefits admissible 
under the said Scheme, 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the 
amounts payable under the scheme be less than the amount 
that would be payable had the employee been covered under 1 
the said Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to 
the nominee(s)/legal heir(s) of the employee as compensa¬ 
tion. 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insur¬ 
ance Scheme shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner, concerned and 
where any amendment is likely to effect adversely the inte¬ 
rest of the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner shall before giving his approval, give a reasonable 
opportunity to the employee to explain their point of view. 

9. Where for any reason, the employees of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as al¬ 
ready adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to 
the employees under this scheme are reduced in any manner 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

i 

10. Where for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life In¬ 
surance Corporation of India, and the policy ds allowed to 
lapse the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by, the employer in 
payment of premium the responsibility for payment of as¬ 
surance benefits to the nominee(s)/Iegal heir(s) of deceased 
member who would hae been covered under the said 
scheme but for grant of this exemption, shall be that of the 
employer. 

12. Upon the death of the member covered under the 
Group Insurance Scheme this Life Insurance Corporation of 
India shall ensure prompt payment of the sum assured to 
the nominee! s)/legal heir(s) of the deceased member en¬ 
titled for it and in any case within one month from the 
receipt of claims complete in all respects. 


B. N. SOM, 

Central Provident Fund Commissioner

UNIT TRUST OF INDIA 
Bombay, the 21st March 1994 

UT/DBOM/1788A/SPD-78/93-94,—The amendments to 
the provisions of the Growing Corpus Growing income 
Scheme 1994 made under Section 21 of the Unit Truvt of 
India Act, 1963 (52 of 1963) approved by the Executive 
Committee in the Meeting held on 21st January, 1994 are 
published herebelow, 

AMENDMENTS TO THE PROVISIONS OF GROWINO 
CORPUS GROWING INCOME SCHEME 1994 

1. Tn Clause No. Ill under Principal Objective, the second 
para is modified as follows : 

tL 60% of all capital appreciation that is booked will go 
lowards investment in fixed income securities. No. more 
than 409L, of the fund will he invested in equity and equity 
related instruments which will be added to the principal by 
way of bonus units after contributing towards income pay¬ 
ment wherever necessary. The issue of bonus units will be 
in multiples of IQ units without any fractions." 

2, Sub-clause (a) of clause VI under Investment Objective 
is modified as follows 

"It shall be the endeavour of the Scheme to invest the 
capital after defraying all initial, preoperative and opera¬ 
tional expenses in securities issued by companies listed/ 
to be listed on the recognized stock exchanges in India. 

Upto 40 r /o of the fund will be invested in equity and 
equity related instruments and balance in Debt instru¬ 
ments and money market instruments. 

Investment as above will be made within the overall 
limits specified in the Regulations, 1964."
